## ०१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/13 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ वै᳓ [[य᳓दहरस्य|य᳓द᳓हरस्य]] श्वो᳙ ऽग्न्याधे᳓यं स्या᳓त्त᳓द᳓हर्दि᳓वैवा᳙श्नीयात् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He (the sacrificer) should take his food during the day-time itself on that day, on the morrow of which the Agnyādheya (setting up of the fires) is going to be.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स वै यदहरस्य᳘ श्वो ऽग्न्याधे᳘यꣳ स्यात्तद᳘हर्दि᳘वै᳘वाश्नीयात्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/19 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

ते᳓ हैनमेत᳓द्विदुरग्नी᳓ [[आधास्य᳓मानमेष|आधास्य᳓मानमेष᳓]] नः प्रात᳓र्यष्टे᳓ति त᳓स्य वि᳓श्वे देवा᳓ गृहा᳓नभ्या᳓गच्छन्ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

They know this about him who is going to install the two fires. "This person is going to worship us (perform sacrifice to us) in the morning." All the gods repair to his residence.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ते᳘ हैनमेत᳘द्विदुरग्नी᳘ आधास्य᳘मानमेष नः प्रात᳘र्यष्टे᳘ति त᳘स्य वि᳘श्वे देवा᳘ गृहा᳘नभ्या᳘गच्छन्ति᳘
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓स्मादुपवसथ᳓स्त᳓न्न्वा᳓ अ᳓नवकॢप्तं यो᳓ मनुष्या᳙न्व᳓सतो ऽन्यश्ना᳓द᳓नवकॢप्तमि᳓दु त᳓दसद्यो᳓ देवा᳓न्व᳓सतो ऽन्यश्ना᳓त्त᳓स्मान्ना᳙श्नीया᳓दि᳓त्याहु᳓स्त᳓दु का᳓ममेवा᳙श्नीयात् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Hence it is called 'upavasatha' (a day of fasting, a day of preparation, a day of remaining near). "It is indeed improper that a person takes his food while people remaining (with him) have not had their food. Surely, it is not right that a person should eat before the gods who remain (with him) have had their food. Therefore he (the sacrificer) should not take his food". So they say. But one may eat (at will).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘स्मादुपवसथस्तन्न्वा अ᳘नवक्लृप्तं यो᳘ मनु᳘ष्यान्व᳘सतो ऽन्यश्नाद᳘नवक्लृप्तमि᳘दु त᳘दसद्यो᳘ देवान्व᳘सतो ऽन्यश्नात्त᳘स्मा᳘न्नाश्नीयादि᳘त्याहुस्त᳘दु का᳘ममे᳘वाश्नीयात्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/20 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

न᳓ वै᳓ व्रतचर्या᳓नाहिताग्नेरस्ति मानुषो᳓ न्वा᳓ एष᳓ ता᳓वद्या᳓वदग्नी᳓ [[नाधत्ते᳓|ना᳓धत्ते᳓]] त᳓स्मादु का᳓ममेवा᳙श्नीयात्॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

There is verily no observance of ritual restriction (abstinence) for one who has not set up the holy fires. As long as he does not set up the two fires, he is indeed a 'man' (a lay man). Therefore he may surely eat at will.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

न वै᳘ व्रतचर्या᳘नाहिताग्नेरस्ति मानुषो न्वा᳘ एष ता᳘वद्या᳘वदग्नी᳘ नाधत्ते त᳘स्मादु का᳘ममे᳘वाश्नीयात् ॥१॥
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः - 1/20 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

- न᳓ (*RV*-2095, *TS*-758, *AV*-649, *TB*-429, *TK*-53, *KS*-139, *MS*-806)

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓दु त᳓था न᳓ कुर्याद्य᳓द्यस्या᳓ज᳓ उ᳓पकॢप्तः स्या᳓च्छ्वो᳓ भूत᳓ एवै᳙नमग्नी᳓धे सं᳓ स्थितौ दद्यात्ते᳓नैव᳓ तं᳓ का᳓ममाप्नोति य᳓स्त᳓त्र का᳓मस्त᳓स्मात्त᳓न्ना᳓द्रियेत॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The goat is intended for Agni and it is for the fullness of Agni. One need not do it that way. If a goat is meant (prepared) for this (fire), he (the sacrificer) may give it to the 'Agnidh' the next day itself for (at) the completion (of the ritual). By that itself he secures that wish which is there (regarding the offering of the goat). Therefore, one need not favour it (the tying up of the goat, the previous night).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दु त᳘था न᳘ कुर्याद्य᳘द्यस्याज उ᳘पक्लृप्तः स्याच्छ्वो᳘ भूत᳘ ए᳘वैनमग्नी᳘धे स᳘ꣳ᳘ स्थितौ दद्यात्ते᳘नैव तं का᳘ममाप्नोति यस्त᳘त्र का᳘मस्त᳘स्मात्तन्ना᳘द्रियेत ॥२॥
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓दु त᳓था न᳓ कुर्याद्य᳓द्वा᳓ अस्यर्त्वि᳓जश्चा᳓नृत्विजश्चास्मि᳓न्व᳓सन्तो ऽस्य᳓ कु᳓ल एतां᳓ रा᳓त्रिमश्न᳓न्ति ते᳓नैव᳓ तं᳓ का᳓ममाप्नोति य᳓स्त᳓त्र का᳓मस्त᳓स्मात्त᳓न्ना᳓द्रियेत॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

For, he achieves the desire that may be there (of propitiating the metres or Vedas) by the very fact that the priests and non-priests staying in his house eat there that night. Therefore, he need not care for it (the practice of cooking special soft food for four priests).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दु त᳘था न᳘ कुर्याद्यद्वा᳘ अस्यर्त्वि᳘जश्वा᳘नृत्विजश्चास्मिन्व᳘सन्तो ऽस्य कु᳘ल एताꣳ रा᳘त्रिमश्न᳘न्ति ते᳘नैव तं का᳘ममाप्नोति यस्त᳘त्र का᳘मस्त᳘स्मात्तन्ना᳘द्रियेत ॥३॥
</details>

## ०४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/26 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓स्य सर्पिरासे᳓चनं कृत्वा᳓ सर्पि᳓रासि᳓च्या᳓श्वत्थीस्तिस्रः᳓ समि᳓धो घृते᳓नान्व᳓ज्य समि᳓द्वतीभिर्घृत᳓वतीभिर् ऋग्भि᳓रभ्या᳓दधाति शमीगर्भ᳓मिद᳓माप्नुम [[इति|इ᳓ति]] व᳓दन्तस्त᳓दु त᳓था न᳓ कुर्यात् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Scooping out a depression in it (the soft rice-food) for ghee, pouring ghee in it and smearing three sticks of Aśvattha wood with ghee, they put them in the fire with (uttering) (three) Rks (metrical chants) containing the words 'Śamit' and 'Ghṛta', (specific mantras) and saying, "We obtain this (fire) that is within (in the womb of) the Śamī (wood)".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘स्य सर्पिरासे᳘चनं कृत्वा᳘ सर्पि᳘रासिच्या᳘श्वत्थीस्तिस्रः᳘ समि᳘धो घृते᳘नान्व᳘ज्य समि᳘द्वतीमिघृत᳘वतीमिर्ऋृग्भि᳘रभ्या᳘दधाति शमीगर्भ᳘मिद᳘माप्रुम इति व᳘दन्तस्त᳘दु त᳘था न᳘ कुर्यात्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/19 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

संवत्सरं᳓ ह [[तथा|त᳓था]] पुरा᳙भ्या᳓दध्यात्ते᳓न ह तं᳓ का᳓ममाप्नुयाद्य᳓स्त᳓त्र का᳓मस्त᳓स्मात्त᳓न्ना᳓द्रियेत॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

One need not do that way. For, one should so put the kindling sticks on the fire, earlier for a year (daily, prior to the ādhana) and by that indeed will he achieve that desire that is therein. Therefore one need not have concern for that (practice of putting the three special sticks).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

संव त्सर᳘ꣳ᳘ ह तथा पु᳘राभ्या᳘दध्यात्ते᳘न ह तं का᳘ममाप्नुयाद्यस्त᳘त्र का᳘मस्त᳘स्मात्तन्ना᳘द्रियेत ॥४॥
</details>

## ०५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/18 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓दु होवाचे᳓न्द्रद्युम्नो भाल्लवेयो᳓ [[य᳓थान्य᳓त्करिष्य᳓न्नन्य᳓त्कु᳓र्यादि᳓ति|य᳓थान्य᳓त्करिष्य᳓न्नन्य᳓त्कुर्या᳓दि᳓ति]] य᳓थान्य᳓द्वदिष्य᳓न्नन्य᳓द्व᳓देदि᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

About this, Indradyumna Bhāllaveya said, "That one (the sacrificer) should cook this rice-food, surely seems to be a wrong thing and it is like this that one (actually) does something intending to do something else, intending to say something may say something else and intending to go along a (particular) path may go along a different way (with action and intention being at variance with each other). Therefore one need not care for it".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दु होवाचे᳘न्द्रद्युम्नो भाल्लवेयो य᳘थान्य᳘त्करिष्य᳘न्नन्य᳘त्कु᳘र्यादि᳘ति य᳘थान्य᳘द्वदिष्य᳘न्नन्यद्व᳘देदि᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः - 1/18 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/47 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

य᳓थान्ये᳓न पथै᳙ष्य᳓न्नन्यं᳓ प᳓न्थानं प्रतिप᳓द्येतैव᳓मेत᳓द्य᳓ एत᳓मोदनं᳓ प᳓चाद᳓पराद्धिरिव वै᳓ सा᳓ त᳓स्मात्त᳓न्ना᳓द्रियेते᳓त्य᳓नवकॢप्तमु वाव᳓ त᳓द्य᳓द्य᳓स्मिन्नृचा᳓ वा य᳓जुषा वा᳓हुतिं वा जुहुयुः᳓ समि᳓धं [[वाभ्यादध्युर्यतं᳓|वाभ्या᳓दध्युर्यतं᳓]] त᳓तो᳓ऽनु वा गम᳓येयुः ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

It is indeed (improper) wrong that they (the sacrificers) should either extinguish (that fire) or take it to the south so that it will be 'Anvāhāryapacana' (Dakṣiṇāgni)—that fire wherein they put the oblation or put a stick with (the utterance of) a Rk or Yajus (earlier). But really, they either extinguish or take it to the south that it may become (serve as) "Anvāhāryapacana". (The preparation of a fire for cooking of the soft rice-food the previous night itself and putting ghee and Samit in it with chants and extinguishing the sanctified fire is improper. Improper is it also to convert it into Dakṣiṇāgni, since it is a fire prepared before the actual agnyādheya.)
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘थान्ये᳘न प᳘थैष्य᳘न्नन्यं प᳘न्थानं प्रतिप᳘द्येतैव᳘मेतद्य᳘ एत᳘मोदनं प᳘चाद᳘पराद्विरिव वै सा त᳘स्मात्तन्ना᳘द्रियेतेत्य᳘नवक्लृप्तमु वाव तद्यद्य᳘स्मिन्नृचा᳘ वा य᳘जुषा वा᳘हुतिं वा जुहुयुः᳘ समि᳘धं वाभ्यादध्युर्यतं ततो᳘ऽनु वा गम᳘येयुः
</details>

## ०६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓दाहुर्जागृया᳓देतां᳓ रा᳓त्रिमि᳓ति जा᳓ग्रति देवा᳓ देवा᳓नेवै᳙त᳓दुपा᳓वर्तते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

With regard to this (conduct on the previous night), they say, "He (the sacrificer) should keep awake during this (entire) night".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दाहुर्जागृया᳘देताꣳ रा᳘त्रिमि᳘ति जा᳘ग्रति देवा᳘ देवा᳘ने᳘वैत᳘दुपा᳘वर्तते᳘
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/15 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

सो᳙ ऽभि᳓श्रान्त᳓तरस्तपस्वि᳓तरः स᳓ [[देवतरो|देव᳓तरो]] ऽग्नी᳓ आ᳓धत्त इ᳓ति त᳓दु का᳓ममेव᳓ स्वप्यात् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The gods keep awake. This means that he approaches the gods by this and he sets up (the next day) the two fires becoming more subdued, more disciplined (with greater austerity) and becoming more godly.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

सो ऽभि᳘श्रान्त᳘तरस्तपस्वि᳘तरः स᳘ देवतरो ऽग्नी आ᳘धत्त इ᳘ति त᳘दु का᳘ममेव᳘ स्वप्यात्
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (4/21 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

न᳓ वै᳓ [[व्रतचर्या᳓नहिता᳓ग्नेरस्ति|व्रतचर्या᳓नहिता᳓ग्ने᳓रस्ति]] मानुषो᳓ न्वा᳓ एष᳓ [[ता᳓वद्या᳓वदग्नी|ता᳓वद्या᳓वदग्नी᳓]] ना᳓धत्ते᳓ त᳓स्मादु का᳓ममेव᳓ स्वप्यात्॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

However, he may sleep as he pleases. For there is no observance of vow (ritualistic restriction) for one who has not (yet) set up the holy fires. He remains verily a man as long as he does not set up the two fires. Therefore he may sleep at will.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

न वै᳘ व्रतचर्या᳘नहिताग्नेरस्ति मानुषो न्वा᳘ एष ता᳘वद्या᳘वदग्नी᳘ नाधत्ते त᳘स्मादु का᳘ममेव᳘ स्वप्यात् ॥६॥
</details>

## ०७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓द्धै᳓के᳓ ऽनुदिते मथित्त्वो᳙द्य᳓न्तमु᳓पासत उ᳓दिते प्रा᳓ञ्चमु᳓द्धरिष्याम इ᳓त्यहोरात्र᳓योः परिगृहीत᳓ये प्राणोदान᳓योर्म᳓नसश्च वाच᳓श्चे᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Now, some churn (the fire) (produce the fire by ceremonial churning of two prescribed pieces of wood) before sunrise and then worship the rising Sun, thinking, "After the Sun has risen we shall take it (the kindled fire) eastward, for securing (the benefit of) (both) day and night, the inhaled breath and the exhaled breath and the mind and speech." One should not do it that way.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तद्धैके᳘ ऽनुदिते मथि᳘त्त्वोद्य᳘न्तमु᳘पासत उ᳘दिते प्रा᳘ञ्चमु᳘द्धरिष्याम इ᳘त्यहोरात्र᳘योः परिगृहीत᳘ये प्राणोदान᳘योर्म᳘नसश्च वाचश्चे᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

य᳓ उ ह त᳓मु᳓दित आहवनी᳓यं म᳓न्थेत्स᳓ ह त᳓त्प᳓र्याप्नुयात्॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He churns before sunrise and then takes it to the east. (So the two fires are only nocturnal). Verily he who churns the Ahavanīya after the Sun has risen, will obtain that (benefit of setting up the fires).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘ उ ह तमु᳘दित आहवनी᳘यं म᳘न्थेत्स᳘ ह तत्प᳘र्याप्नुयात् ॥७॥
</details>

## ०८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓दु क᳓ उभौ᳓ निर्म᳓न्थ्यौ कुर्वीता᳓हर्देवा᳓ रा᳓त्रिः पित᳓रो᳓ ऽनपहतपाप्मानः पित᳓रो न᳓ पाप्मा᳓नम᳓पहते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then who will make the two churned fires (before sunrise)? Therefore one should make the two fires by churning (after sunrise only). The day is the gods, the night the manes. The manes do not have their evils dispelled.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दु क᳘ उभौ᳘ निर्म᳘न्थ्यौ कुर्वीता᳘हर्देवा रा᳘त्रिः पितरो᳘ ऽनपहतपाप्मानः पित᳘रो न᳘ पाप्मा᳘नम᳘पहते
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

म᳓र्त्याः पित᳓रः पुरा᳓ हा᳓युषो म्रियते यो᳓ऽनुदित आधत्ते᳓॥

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He (the sacrificer who kindles the fire before sunrise) does not dispel his (own) evil. The manes are mortal. He who sets up (the fires) when the Sun has not risen dies before his (normal) life-span.
</details>

## १०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/25 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[त᳓दाहुर्यन्न᳓र्चा᳓|त᳓दाहुर्य᳓न्न᳓र्चा᳓]] न᳓ य᳓जुषा न᳓ सा᳓म्नाग्नि᳓राधीय᳓ते [[केन᳓|के᳓न]] त्व᳙ग्नि᳓रा᳓धीयत इ᳓ति स᳓ ह ब्रूयाद्ब्र᳓ह्मण एव᳓ ब्र᳓ह्मणे᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

They say, "As the fire is not set up with (the chanting of) a Rā nor with Yajus nor with Sāman, then with what is the fire set up?" He (the sacrificer) should indeed say, "Surely it is of Brahman, with Brahman" (it is set up) with Brahman (the symbolic formula of the Brahman only). Indeed "Speech is Brahman". Its truth itself is Brahman.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दाहुर्य᳘न्नर्चा न य᳘जुषा न सा᳘म्नाग्नि᳘राधीय᳘ते केन᳘ त्वग्निरा᳘धीयत इ᳘ति स᳘ ह ब्रूयाद्ब्र᳘ह्मण एव ब्र᳘ह्मणे᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/21 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

वा᳓ग्वै᳓ ब्र᳓ह्म त᳓स्याः सत्य᳓मेव᳓ ब्र᳓ह्मा ता᳓ एताः᳓ सत्य᳓मेव᳓ व्या᳓हृतयो [[भूर्भुवः᳓|भू᳓र्भुवः᳓]] स्व᳙रि᳓ति सत्ये᳓न है᳓वास्या᳓हितौ᳓ भ᳓वतः॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

These vyāhytis (mystic utterances) Bhūh, Bhuvah, Svah are only 'Truth'. His (the sacrificer's) two fires are verily set up with 'Truth'.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

वाग्वै ब्र᳘ह्म त᳘स्याः सत्य᳘मेव ब्र᳘ह्मा ता᳘ एताः᳘ सत्य᳘मेव व्या᳘हृतयो भूर्भुवः᳘ स्वरि᳘ति सत्ये᳘न है᳘वास्या᳘हितौ भ᳘वतः ॥१०॥
</details>

## ११

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/24 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

भू᳓रि᳓ति वै᳓ प्रजा᳓पतिरिमा᳓मजनयद्भु᳓व [[इत्यन्त᳓रिक्षं|इ᳓त्यन्त᳓रिक्षं]] स्व᳙रि᳓ति दि᳓वमेता᳓वद्वा᳓ इदं᳓ या᳓वदिमे᳓ लोकाः᳓ स᳓र्वेण हैवा᳙स्या᳓हितौ भवतः᳓॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Indeed Prajāpati generated this (earth) by (uttering) (the mystic syllable) 'Bhūh' the middle region (between earth and heaven) by 'Bhuvah and the sky by Svah'. As far as these worlds are thus far is this (Universe). The two fires become established with the whole of this (Universe).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

भूरि᳘ति वै᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिरिमा᳘मजनयद्भु᳘व इत्यन्त᳘रिक्ष᳘ꣳ᳘ स्वरि᳘ति दि᳘वमेता᳘वद्वा᳘ इदं या᳘वदिमे᳘ लोका: स᳘र्वेण है᳘वास्या᳘हितौ भवतः॥११॥
</details>

## १२

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/25 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

भू᳓रि᳓ति वै᳓ प्रजा᳓पतिर्ब्र᳓ह्माजनयद्भु᳓व इ᳓ति [[क्ष᳓त्रं|क्षत्रं᳓]] स्व᳙रि᳓ति वि᳓शमेता᳓वद्वा᳓ इदं᳓ या᳓वद्ब्र᳓ह्म क्षत्रं᳓ वि᳓ट् स᳓र्वेण हैवा᳙स्या᳓हितौ भवतः᳓॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Prajāpati created Brāhmaṇa (the Brāhmaṇa class/priesthood) by (uttering) 'Bhūh'; the Kṣatra (Kṣatriya class) by Bhuvah and viṭ (Vaiṣya) by Svah. So much is this—as much as are Brāhmaṇa, Kṣatra and viṭ. Indeed the two fires are set up with the whole of this.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

भूरि᳘ति वै᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिर्ब्र᳘ह्माजनयद्भु᳘व इ᳘ति क्ष᳘त्रꣳ᳘ स्वरि᳘ति वि᳘शमेता᳘वद्वा᳘ इदं या᳘वद्ब्र᳘ह्म क्षत्रं विट् स᳘र्वेण है᳘वास्या᳘हितौ भवतः॥१२॥
</details>

## १३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

भू᳓रि᳓ति वै᳓ प्रजा᳓पतिरात्मा᳓नमजनयद्भु᳓व इ᳓ति प्रजां᳓ स्व᳙रि᳓ति पशू᳓नेता᳓वद्वा᳓ इदं᳓ या᳓वदात्मा᳓ प्रजा᳓ पश᳓वः स᳓र्वेण है᳓वास्या᳓हितौ भवतः᳓॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Prajāpati verily generated Ātman (the self) with Bhūh; the excellent creature(s) (human race) with 'Bhuvah' and the animals with 'Svah'. So much indeed is this, viz., as much as the self, humanity and animals.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

भूरि᳘ति वै᳘ प्रजा᳘पतिरात्मा᳘नमजनयद्भु᳘व इ᳘ति प्रजा᳘ꣳ᳘ स्वरि᳘ति पशू᳘नेता᳘वद्वा᳘ इदं या᳘वदात्मा᳘ प्रजा᳘ पश᳘वः स᳘र्वेण है᳘वास्या᳘हितौ भवतः॥१३॥
</details>

## १४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ भू᳓र्भु᳓व इ᳓त्येतै᳓स्त्रिभि᳓रक्ष᳓रैर्गा᳓र्हपत्यमा᳓दधाति द्वे᳓ प᳓रिशिनष्टि ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He (the sacrificer) installs the Gārhapatya (fire) with (uttering) these three syllables 'Bhūrbhuvah' and (leaves out) keep (unused) two (syllables) for the sake of 'freshness' (the state of not becoming stale with lapse of time).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स भूर्भु᳘व इ᳘त्येतै᳘स्त्रिभि᳘रक्ष᳘रैर्गा᳘र्हपत्यमा᳘दधाति द्वे प᳘रिशिनष्टि
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अयातयाम᳓तायै के᳓न ह्य᳙मु᳓मादध्या᳓द्य᳓त्स᳓र्वैरादध्या᳓त्ते᳓ एव᳓ द्वे᳓ इ᳓तराणि त्री᳓ण्यन्वा᳓प्यायन्ते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

With what will he set up that (the Āhavanīya) if he should establish (the Gārhapatya) with all (the five syllables)? He should set up (the Āhavanīya) with all (the five syllables).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/9 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

ते᳓नो ता᳓न्य᳓यातयामानि भवन्ति ता᳓न्यष्टा᳓वक्ष᳓राणि [[संपद्यन्ते|सं᳓पद्यन्ते]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Those very two (syllables that were left out); the other three (once used) are strengthened (refreshed). Thereby they become fresh (not old and stale).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

ते᳘नो तान्य᳘यातयामानि भवन्ति ता᳘न्यष्टा᳘वक्ष᳘राणि संपद्यन्ते
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः - 1/9 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

- ते᳓नो (*AV*-1, *SB-M*-44)
- ता᳓नि (*TS*-37/4763, *RV*-34/3136, *TB*-39/2709, *TK*-2/363, *AV*-27/3165, *SB-M*-245/20457, *VS-M*-5/768, *VS-K*-0/395, *KS*-26/1225, *MS*-50/5550)

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त्री᳓णीमा᳓नि प᳓ञ्चामू᳓न्यष्टा᳓क्षरा गायत्री᳓ गायत्र᳓मग्ने᳓श्छ᳓न्दः स्वे᳓नैवै᳙नं त᳓च्छ᳓न्दसा᳓धत्ते॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

They form eight syllables (for the two fires together)—these five and those three. Indeed Gāyatrī consists of eight syllables. Gāyatra is the metre of Agni. He (the sacrificer), thus sets up this (fire) with its own metre.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त्री᳘णीमा᳘नि प᳘ञ्चामू᳘न्यष्टा᳘क्षरा गायत्री᳘ गायत्र᳘मग्नेश्छ᳘न्दः स्वे᳘नै᳘वैनं तच्छ᳘न्दसा᳘धत्ते ॥१४॥
</details>

## १५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

देवा᳓नु ह वा᳓ अग्नी᳓ आधि᳓त्समानानसुररक्षसा᳓नि ररक्षुः ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

The Asuras and Rākṣasas verily kept away (prevented) the gods who wished to set up the two fires. (They said to the gods), "Agni will not be produced (be born); you are not going to set up the two fires".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

देवा᳘नु ह वा᳘ अग्नी᳘ आधि᳘त्समानानसुररक्षसा᳘नि ररक्षु᳘ः
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/31 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[ना᳙ग्नि᳓रजिनिष्य᳓ते|ना᳙ग्नि᳓रजिनिष्यते]] ना᳙ग्नी᳓ आ᳓धास्यध्व इ᳓ति त᳓स्माद्र᳓क्षांसि त᳓तो ह देवा᳓ एतं᳓ व᳓ज्रं ददृशुर्य᳓द᳓श्वं त᳓मुद᳓यच्छंस्ते᳓न नाष्ट्रा᳓ र᳓क्षांस्यपह᳓त्य त᳓स्या᳓भयेऽनाष्ट्रे᳓ निवाते᳙ ऽग्नि᳓रजायत ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Therefore, they became (came to be called) 'Rākṣasas' (preventees). Then indeed did the gods see the thunderbolt, viz. the horse. They raised it up and on their destroying the destructive Rākṣasas, fire arose (was produced) in its safe shelter that was free from fear.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

नाग्नि᳘रजिनिष्य᳘ते᳘ नाग्नी आ᳘धास्यध्व इ᳘ति त᳘स्माद्र᳘क्षाꣳसि त᳘तो ह देवा᳘ एतं व᳘ज्रं ददृशुर्यद᳘श्वं त᳘मुद᳘यच्छꣳस्ते᳘न नाष्ट्रा र᳘क्षाꣳस्यपह᳘त्य तस्या᳘भयेऽनाष्ट्रे᳘ निवा᳘ते ऽग्नि᳘रजायत
</details>

## १७

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/33 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

स᳓ पूर्ववा᳓ट् स्याद्वीर्यं᳙ वा᳓ अ᳓श्व एष᳓ वा᳓ अ᳓परिमितं वीर्य᳙मभि᳓ वर्धते यः᳓ पूर्ववा᳓ङ्य᳓दि न᳓ पूर्ववा᳓ळ᳓पि य᳓ एवं᳓ [[क᳓श्चित्स्या᳓द्यदि|क᳓श्चित्स्या᳓द्य᳓दि]] ना᳓श्वो᳓ [[ऽप्यनड्वान्त्स्या᳓दे᳓ष|ऽप्यनड्वा᳓न्त्स्या᳓दे᳓ष]] ए᳓वानमल्लु᳓हो बन्धु᳓ता॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

It (the horse) should be one that (moves ahead) (eastward) is a leader (foremost of horses). Indeed 'virility' is the horse. This (horse) is verily (represents) limitless vigour. He who is the leader (who goes in the fore front—who moves eastward) grows greatly. If there is no leader (leading horse) available, any one (any horse that is available) will do. If even (an ordinary) horse is not available, there may be an ox (an ox will serve the purpose). This indeed is the relationship of the ox (with the horse).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

स᳘ पूर्ववा᳘ट् स्याद्वी᳘र्यं वा अ᳘श्व एष वा अ᳘परिमितं वी᳘र्यमभि᳘ वर्धते यः᳘ पूर्ववाङ्य᳘दि न᳘ पूर्ववाळ᳘पि य᳘ एवं क᳘श्चित्स्याद्य᳘दि᳘ नाश्वो᳘ ऽप्यनड्वा᳘न्त्स्यादे᳘षो ए᳘वानमल्लु᳘हो बन्धु᳘ता॥१७॥
</details>

## १८

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

तं᳓ य᳓त्र प्रा᳓ञ्चमुद्धरिष्य᳓न्स्या᳓त्त᳓द्ब्रूयाद᳓श्वेन पू᳓र्वेण प्रे᳓ही᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Where he (the sacrificer) is going to take it (the fires) on eastward, then he should say, "Move on with the horse ahead". Indeed the horse is the thunderbolt.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तं य᳘त्र प्रा᳘ञ्चमुद्धरिष्यन्स्यात्त᳘द्ब्रूयाद᳘श्वेन पू᳘र्वेण प्रेही᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

व᳓ज्रो वा᳓ अ᳓श्वो व᳓ज्रेणैवै᳙त᳓त्पुर᳓स्तान्नाष्ट्रा᳓ र᳓क्षांस्यपघ्न᳓न्प्रमिन्द᳓न्नेत्य᳓थैत᳓म᳓भये ऽनाष्ट्रे᳓ निवा᳓ते हरति तं᳓ त᳓था हरेत् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Verily destroying the devastating demons (Rākṣasas) in front with the thunderbolt itself and breaking them up, he (the sacrificer) moves and then takes it (the fire) into the safe and fearless shelter. One should take it in such a manner that it will turn (westward) (backward) towards the sacrificer.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

व᳘ज्रो वा अ᳘श्वो व᳘ज्रेणै᳘वैत᳘त्पुर᳘स्तान्नाष्ट्रा र᳘क्षाꣳस्यपघ्न᳘न्प्रमिन्द᳘न्नेत्य᳘थैतम᳘भये ऽनाष्ट्रे᳘᳘ निवा᳘ते हरति तं त᳘था हरेत्
</details>

## १९

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/27 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[एष|एष᳓]] वाव᳓ यज्ञो᳓ य᳓दग्निः᳓ प्रत्य᳓ङ् हैवै᳙नं यज्ञः᳓ प्र᳓विशति तं᳓ क्षिप्रं᳓ यज्ञ᳓ उ᳓पनमत्य᳓थ य᳓स्मादेष᳓ प᳓राङ्भ᳓वति प᳓राङ् हास्माद्यज्ञो᳓ भवति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This fire is indeed the sacrifice. The sacrifice verily enters him (the sacrificer) (westward) backwards. Him the sacrifice quickly approaches. Moreover, the sacrifice turns away from him from whom this (fire) turns away.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एष वाव᳘ यज्ञो य᳘दग्निः᳘ प्रत्य᳘ङ् है᳘वैनं यज्ञः प्र᳘विशति तं᳘ क्षिप्रं᳘ यज्ञ उ᳘पनमत्य᳘थ य᳘स्मादेष प᳘राङ्भ᳘वति प᳘राङ् हास्माद्यज्ञो᳘ भवति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

यो᳓ हैनं त᳓त्रानुव्याह᳓रेत्प᳓राङस्माद्यज्ञो᳙ ऽभूत्नै᳙नं यज्ञ᳓ उ᳓पनंस्यतीती᳙श्वरो᳓ ह त᳓थैव᳓ स्यात् ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

If one, there, should curse him saying, "May the sacrifice turn away from this person, may the lordly sacrifice not approach this person: lordly is it (the sacrifice). (The sacrifice will not approach this person)".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

यो᳘ हैनं त᳘त्रानुव्याह᳘रेत्प᳘राङस्माद्य᳘ज्ञो ऽभू᳘त्रैनं यज्ञ उ᳘पनꣳस्यती᳘तीश्वरो᳘ ह त᳘थैव᳘ स्यात्
</details>

## २०

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/27 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[एष|एष᳓]] वाव᳓ प्राणो᳓ य᳓दग्निः᳓ प्रत्य᳓ङ् हैवै᳙नं प्राणः᳓ प्र᳓विशति स᳓ स᳓र्वमा᳓युरेत्य᳓थ य᳓स्मादेष᳓ प᳓राङ्भ᳓वति प᳓राङ् हास्मात्प्राणो᳓ भवति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This fire indeed is the vital breath. Him (who takes the fire in such a way that it turns back towards him) verily the life-breath enters back. He attains the entire (span of) life. And from whom this (fire) turns away, vital breath also turns away from him.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एष वाव᳘ प्राणो य᳘दग्निः᳘ प्रत्य᳘ङ् है᳘वैनं प्राणः प्र᳘विशति स स᳘र्वमा᳘युरेत्य᳘थ य᳘स्मादेष प᳘राङ्भ᳘वति प᳘राङ् हास्मात्प्राणो᳘ भवति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

## २१

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

एष᳓ उ वाव᳓ यज्ञः᳓ प्रत्य᳓ङ् हैवै᳙नं यज्ञः᳓ प्र᳓विशति तं᳓ क्षिप्रं᳓ यज्ञ᳓ उ᳓पनमत्य᳓थ य᳓स्मादेष᳓ प᳓राङ् भ᳓वति प᳓राङ् हास्माद्यज्ञो᳓ भवति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This (fire) verily is the sacrifice. Indeed, him (the sacrificer) the sacrifice enters backward. Him the sacrifice reaches quickly. And the sacrifice turns away surely from him from whom this (fire) turns away.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

एष᳘ उ वाव᳘ यज्ञः᳘ प्रत्य᳘ङ् है᳘वैनं यज्ञः प्र᳘विशति तं᳘ क्षिप्रं᳘ यज्ञ उ᳘पनमत्य᳘थ य᳘स्मादेषै प᳘राङ् भ᳘वति प᳘राङ् हास्माद्यज्ञो᳘ भवति
</details>

## २३

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓म᳓श्वमा᳓क्रमयति तं᳓ प्रा᳓ञ्चमु᳓त्क्रमयति तं᳓ पु᳓नरा᳓वर्तयति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He (the sacrificer) makes the horse step on it (the fire-altar), then makes it move up towards the east and then makes it turn back again.
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

वीर्यं᳙ वा᳓ अ᳓श्वो वीर्य᳙ एवै᳙नं तदा᳓धत्ते त᳓मु᳓पस्पर्शयति ।

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/17 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

त᳓मु᳓द्यच्छति त᳓मु᳓पस्पर्शयति त᳓मु᳓द्यच्छति तं᳓ तृती᳓येनैव᳓ [[भू᳓र्भुवः᳓|भू᳓र्भु᳓वः]] स्व᳙रित्या᳓धत्ते ।

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He (the sacrificer) makes it (the fire) touch it (the horse's foot-print) and lifts it up (again); he makes it touch it and lifts it up. Only at the third time he establishes it (the fire) chanting 'Bhūrbhuvaḥsvaḥ'.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

तमु᳘द्यच्छति तमु᳘पस्पर्शयति तमु᳘द्यच्छति तं᳘ तृती᳘येनैव भूर्भुवः᳘ स्वरित्या᳘धत्ते
</details>

## २४

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

त᳓दाहुः सकृ᳓देवो᳙पस्पृ᳓श्या᳓थ द्विती᳓येनैवा᳓दध्यादि᳓ति यो᳓ वा᳓ अस्या᳓म᳓प्रतिष्ठितो भार᳓मुद्य᳓च्छती᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

They say, regarding this, "Touching (the horse's footprint) only once, then at the second time one should establish (the fire)".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

त᳘दाहुः सकृ᳘दे᳘वोपस्पृश्या᳘थ द्विती᳘येनैवा᳘दध्यादि᳘ति यो वा᳘ अस्याम᳘प्रतिष्ठितो भार᳘मुद्य᳘च्छती᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/17 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[नैनमु᳓द्यन्तुं|नै᳓नमु᳓द्यन्तुं]] शक्नोति स᳓मेनं शृणाति स᳓ य᳓त्सकृ᳓दुपस्पृश᳓ति त᳓दस्यां᳓ प्रतिष्ठा᳓यां प्र᳓तितिष्ठति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He who, without remaining established (well planted) on this (earth) lifts up a burden (tries to lift up) cannot lift it up. It crushes him. The fact that he touches once (means) he stays firm on this resting place (the earth).
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

नैनमु᳘द्यन्तुꣳ शक्नोति स᳘मेनꣳ शृणाति स य᳘त्सकृ᳘दुपस्पृश᳘ति त᳘दस्यां᳘ प्रतिष्ठा᳘यां प्र᳘तितिष्ठति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

स᳓ प्र᳓तिष्ठितो द्विती᳓येनैव᳓ भू᳓र्भु᳓वः स्व᳙रित्या᳓धत्ते त᳓था न᳓ व्यथते ।

## २५

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

उ᳓ल्मुकान्यु᳓पस्पृशति द्यौ᳓रिव भूम्ना᳓ भू᳓मिरिव वरिम्णे᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He (the sacrificer) touches the burning sticks saying, "Like the sky with abundance, like the earth with girth (in superiority)".
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

उ᳘ल्मुकान्यु᳘पस्पृशति द्यौ᳘रिव भूम्ना भू᳘मिरिव वरिम्णे᳘ति
</details>

<details><summary>पद-पाठः (यान्त्रिकः)</summary>

- भू᳓मिः (*TS*-7/24, *RV*-15/52, *TB*-2/6, *TK*-0/1, *AV*-44/120, *SB-M*-8/19, *VS-M*-5/15, *VS-K*-5/17, *KS*-4/9, *MS*-12/27)

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

य᳓थासौ᳓ द्यौ᳓र्बह्वी᳓ न᳓क्षत्रैरेवं᳓ बहु᳓र्भूयासमि᳓त्येवै᳙त᳓दाह भू᳓मिरिव वरिम्णे᳓ति य᳓थेय᳓मूर्वी᳓ भू᳓मिरेव᳓मुरु᳓र्भूयासमि᳓त्येवै᳙त᳓दाह त᳓स्यास्ते पृथिवि देवयजनि पृष्ठ᳓ इ᳓त्यस्या᳓ ह्ये᳙नं पृष्ठ᳓ आधत्ते᳙ ऽग्नि᳓मन्नाद᳓मन्ना᳓द्याया᳓दध इ᳓ति ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He says verily this, "Just as the yonder sky is plentiful with stars, so may I become plentiful!" By "like the earth with girth"—he says verily this, "Just as this earth is big (vast) may I become big (great)". He (the sacrificer) establishes this (fire) on the back of this (earth). Chanting, "Oh Earth! the one fit for the worship of the Gods! On the back of “Yourself—, I set up Agni the eater of food, for the sake of good food”.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

य᳘थौसौ द्यौ᳘र्बह्वी न᳘क्षत्रैरेवं᳘ बहु᳘र्भूयासमि᳘त्ये᳘वैत᳘दाह भू᳘मिरिव वरिम्णे᳘ति य᳘थेय᳘मूर्वी भू᳘मिरेव᳘मुरु᳘र्भूयासमि᳘त्ये᳘वैत᳘दाह त᳘स्यास्ते पृथिवि देवयजनि पृष्ठ इ᳘त्यस्या᳘ ह्येनं पृष्ठ᳘ आध᳘त्ते ऽग्नि᳘मन्नाद᳘मन्ना᳘द्याया᳘दध इ᳘ति
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः</summary>

अन्नादो᳙ ऽग्नि᳓रन्नादो᳓ भूयासमि᳓त्येवै᳙त᳓दाह सै᳙षा᳙शी᳓स्तां᳓ य᳓दि काम᳓येत जपेद्य᳓द्यु काम᳓येता᳓पि नै᳙वा᳓द्रियेत॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

He says (means) verily this “Agni is the consumer of food. May I become the consumer of food!” This is (a prayer for) blessing. One may mutter it, if he wishes. If he so wishes he may ignore it.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अन्ना᳘दो ऽग्नि᳘रन्नादो᳘ भूयासमि᳘त्ये᳘वैत᳘दाह᳘ सै᳘षाशीस्तां य᳘दि काम᳘येत जपेद्य᳘द्यु काम᳘येता᳘पि᳘ नैवा᳘द्रियेत ॥२५॥
</details>

## २६

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/12 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

अ᳓थ सर्पराज्ञ्या᳓ [[ऋग्भि᳓रु᳓पतिष्ठत᳓|ऋग्भि᳓रु᳓पतिष्ठत]] आयं᳓ गौः᳓ पृ᳓श्निरक्रमीद᳓सदन्मात᳓रं [[पुरः|पुरः᳓]] ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

Then he worships with the Rks pertaining to the ‘Queen of serpents’, (thus), “This spotted bull has come here and has lain down before the mother and before the father, too, on going up to heaven. She, shining (with liking) moves in, breathing out from his breath. The great one has illumined the sky. He shines much in his thirty abodes. Speech is bestowed on the winged one, Ah! with the lustres of everything!” Whatever has not been secured by the installation of this (fire) or by the seasons or by the (gathered) materials or the stars, that very thing, all that one secures by this. One need not care for it.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

अ᳘थ सर्पराज्ञ्या᳘ ऋग्भिरु᳘पतिष्ठत᳘ आयं गौः पृ᳘श्निरक्रमीद᳘सदन्मात᳘रं पुरः ।
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (1/21 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

पित᳓रं च प्रय᳓न्त्स्वः᳙ ।  
अन्त᳓श्चरति रोचना᳙स्य᳓ प्राणा᳓दपानती᳓ ।  
[[व्यख्यन्महिषो᳓|व्य᳓ख्यन्महिषो᳓]] दि᳓वम् ।  
त्रिंश᳓द्धा᳓म वि᳓राजति वा᳓क्पतङ्गा᳓य धीयते ।
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

पित᳘रं च प्रय᳘न्त्स्वः । अन्त᳘श्चरति रोच᳘नास्य᳘ प्राणा᳘दपानती । व्यख्यन्महिषो दि᳘वम् । त्रिꣳशद्धा᳘म वि᳘राजति वा᳘क्पतङ्गा᳘य धीयते ।
</details>

<details open><summary>विश्वास-प्रस्तुतिः (2/27 व्यत्यस्त-स्वराः)</summary>

[[इयं|इयं᳓]] वाव᳓ सर्पराज्ञी᳓ य᳓द्धि᳓ किं᳓ च स᳓र्पति त᳓स्येय᳓मेव᳓ रा᳓ज्ञी स᳓ य᳓देवा᳙स्या᳓माधत्ते᳓ ते᳓नैवा᳙स्यै᳓ष᳓ कृत्स्नः᳓ स᳓र्वः स᳓मृद्ध [[आहितो|आ᳓हितो]] भवति॥
</details>

<details><summary>सर्वाष् टीकाः</summary>

<details><summary>C R Swaminathan</summary>

This (earth) is indeed the Queen of Serpents (for) whatever creeps along of (all) that this (earth) verily is the queen. The fact that he sets up (the fire) on this (earth), verily by that itself, this (fire), entire and full and rich, becomes established.
</details>

<details><summary>मूलम्</summary>

इयं वाव᳘ सर्पराज्ञी यद्धि किं᳘ च स᳘र्पति त᳘स्येय᳘मेव रा᳘ज्ञी स य᳘दे᳘वास्या᳘माधत्ते ते᳘नै᳘वास्यैष᳘ कृत्स्नः स᳘र्वः स᳘मृद्ध आहितो भवति ॥२६॥
</details>
